Validation – Input Data Not Provided

Validation Label 
	I1_InputDataNotProvided
	Message ID
	8233
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	1st level validation
	Section
	All files
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to ensure all mandatory fields are provided.
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
Mandatory field = Null or Blank

Generic validation triggered for all required fields.
Note: ALL mandatory fields have been identified in respective ER Config File.
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	{1} is a required field.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	A mandatory field was not provided in the data file.
	Message Parameters
	1: Input field
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info

Validation – Maximum Mandatory Fields Length

Validation Label 
	I3_MaximumMandatoryFieldsLength
	Message ID
	9519
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	1st level validation
	Section
	All files except Non-Contributory Leaves and Retirement Notice 
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error 
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Generic validation triggered for all text and amount type fields where the length exceeds the max field length. We are not looking at fields where a code or date can be supplied because we have format validations for those already.
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
Core Data
Length(file.Weeks) > MaxLength =5 4
OR
Length(file.LowContributions) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.HighContributions) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.PA) > MaxLength = 5
OR
Length(file.AnnualizedEarnings) > MaxLength = 6
Events
Length(file.Weeks_CurrentYear) > MaxLength = 5 
OR
Length(file.LowContributions_CurrentYear) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.HighContributions_CurrentYear) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.AnnualizedEarnings_CurrentYear) > MaxLength = 6
OR
Length(file.PA_CurrentYear) > MaxLength = 5 
OR
Length(file.Weeks_PreviousYear) > MaxLength = 5 
OR
Length(file.LowContributions_PreviousYear) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.HighContributions_PreviousYear) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.AnnualizedEarnings_PreviousYear) > MaxLength = 6
OR
Length(file.PA_PreviousYear) > MaxLength = 5 

In Enrolments
Length(file.LastName) > MaxLength = 40
OR
Length(file.MiddleInitial) > MaxLength = 2
OR
Length(file.FirstName) > MaxLength = 40
OR
Length(file.AnnualizedEarnings) > MaxLength = 6
OR
Length(file.TelephoneNumber) > MaxLength = 15
OR
Length(file.EmailAddress) > MaxLength = 80
OR
Length(file.AddressLine1) > MaxLength = 40
OR
Length(file.AddressLine2) > MaxLength = 40
OR
Length(file.AddressLine3) > MaxLength = 40
OR
Length(file.City) > MaxLength = 40
OR
Length(file.PostalCodeZipCode) > MaxLength = 12


Retro
Length(file.RetroLowContributions) > MaxLength =8 7
OR
Length(file.RetroHighContributions) > MaxLength = 8 

Contributory Leaves
Length(file.TotalWeeksPaid) > MaxLength = 5 
OR
Length(file.LowContributions) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.HighContributions) > MaxLength = 8 
OR
Length(file.PctOfNormalEarnings) > MaxLength = 6 
OR
Length(file.AmendedPA) > MaxLength = 7

Member Basic Info 
Length(file.LastName) > MaxLength = 40
OR
Length(file.MiddleInitial) > MaxLength = 2
OR
Length(file.FirstName) > MaxLength = 40

Previous Year Adjustment
Length(file.Weeks) > MaxLength = 5 
OR
Length(file.LowContri butions) > MaxLength = 10 
OR
Length(file.HighContri butions) > MaxLength = 10 
OR
Length(file.AmendedPA) > MaxLength = 5
Retro Calculator
Length(file.RetroEarnings) > MaxLength = 9 
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	The field {File.FieldName} exceeds the maximum acceptable length of {Max Length} characters (including decimal point, if applicable).
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The field {File.FieldName} exceeds the maximum acceptable length of {Max Length} characters (including decimal point, if applicable).
	Message Parameters
	0: File.Applicable field
file.LastName
file.FirstName
file.Weeks
file.LowContributions
file.HighContributions
file.PA
file.AnnualizedEarnings
file.Weeks_CurrentYear
file.LowContributions_CurrentYear
file.HighContributions_CurrentYear
file.AnnualizedEarnings_CurrentYear
file.PA_CurrentYear
file.Weeks_PreviousYear
file.LowContributions_PreviousYear
file.HighContributions_PreviousYear
file.AnnualizedEarnings_PreviousYear
file.PA_PreviousYear
file.MiddleInitial
file.TelephoneNumber
file.EmailAddress
file.AddressLine1
file.AddressLine2
file.AddressLine3
file.City
file.PostalCodeZipCode
file.RetroLowContributions
file.RetroHighContributions
file.TotalWeeksPaid
file.PctOfNormalEarnings
file.AmendedPA
file.RetroEarnings

	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	Only required for specific fields and specific config files.
Eg. Required for Enrollments config file
Eg. 1: First Name field does not exceed 40 characters for enrolment.
Eg.2: Address Line 1 does not exceed 40 characters.
Do we need this validation? If so, should this validation only be run against mandatory fields?
Should this validation only be run against fields with a Data Type = Character?  The reason being there is no real input validation for this.
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	CS 20180124:
R14.8.1: Update to wording of message to add “if applicable” at the end because not all fields will contain a decimal.

CS 20180112:
Updates made in R14.8:
Change in wording approved by Alias in email of Jan 11, 2018 (added “including decimal point” to the end of current message).
Update to max length in validation logic to include decimal space for decimal values.
Corrected max PA length in Events file to max 5 (and not 6)

CS 20171115:
Added validation logic applicable to Retro Calculator.

CS 20170425:
Number of characters includes the decimal places.
LastName and FirstName will only be verified in Enrolments and MBI.
I7 covers the check on the decimal places.
Ready for sign-off
AD 20170421:
Further review/discussion with MS is required before sign-off.  
CS 20170410:
Should apply to all files and all text and amount fields (whether mandatory or not)
Carina to update the Validation Logic
	

Validation – Invalid Date

Validation Label 
	I5_ InvalidDate
	Message ID
	825
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	1st level validation
	Section
	Enrolments, Member Basic Info, Core Data, Contributory Leaves, Close Leaves, Events (Final Data), Non-Contributory Leaves, Retirement Notice
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to ensure that the correct format has been provided for all data types = "Date".
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
Format of value provided is:
not numeric
and/or
date format ≠ MMDDYYYY 
and/or
invalid value for MM, DD, YYYY

Note: ALL "Date" data types have been identified in respective ER Config File.

	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	{1} is invalid. Date must be in MMDDYYYY format.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The date value is invalid. Date must be in MMDDYYYY format.
	Message Parameters
	1: Input file value
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	N/A
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	YKN 20180720:
Added section Close Leaves.MR 20180720:
XMO Validation brought into Full Office spec.

Validation Label 
	I7_InvalidDecimal
	Message ID
	6503 / 6642

	Reject the member in the input file if :
Format of value provided is:
Not numeric
and/or

Validation – Valid Year Provided Previous Year Adjustment

Validation Label 
	I49a_ValidYearProvided_PYA (Removed)
	Message ID
	 5334
	Validation Tool

	Level

	Section
	Previous Year Adjustment

	Public

	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if:
RY < VYT
WHERE
RY = File.AdjustmentYear
AND
VYT = Current Year - 10 
Current Year = Processing Year on the Portal (i.e. Start Date)

	Tolerance
	Default Tolerance = [(Current Year) – (10 years)]
	DataImport Message
	Previous Year Adjustments cannot be more than 10 years prior to processing year. Please correct the year, or contact HOOPP if Adjustment Year is correct.

	Value should be a configurable parameter

	CS 20171218:
HOOPP (Alias and Clare) has confirmed that this message can be permanently removed. The message was only created to follow HOOPPs old process which could not handle more than 10 years of prior year data. There is no limitation on the MS dataimport side therefore this validation is no longer required.

Validation – Retro Year VS Year Retro Paid

Validation Label 
	I54_RetroYear VSYearRetroPaid
	Message ID
	 5972
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	1st level validation
	Section
	Retro and Retro Calculator
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Retro Year must be less than the Year Retro Paid.  

	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if:
File.YearRetro >= File.YearRetroPaid
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	The Retro Year cannot be greater than or equal to the Year Retro Paid. Please revise the retro paid in {1} reported for {0}.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The Retro Year cannot be greater than or equal to the Year Retro Paid. 
	Message Parameters
	0: File.YearRetro
1: File.YearRetroPaid
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	a. If the Retro Year = Year Retro Paid = Current Year the ER should wait and report contributions as part of MDC data for the current year.  They should remit contributions as part of the regular contributions for the current year.
b. If the Retro Year = Year Retro Paid = MDC Year the ER should report contributions as part of MDC data.  Reconciliation process will determine if additional payments are required.   
c. If the Retro Year = Year Retro Paid = (Year < MDC Year) the ER should report contributions as PYA to previously reported regular contributions.  
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	CS 20171115:
Added validation logic applicable to Retro Calculator.

HOOPP to change Master Val Number to an input message (Ixx)

	CS 20170320: 
During the workshop discussion Alias brought up validation B146 which is doing the same check that B1 was intended for. As such validation B1 should be removed.
During the workshop discussion Gregory Pierre brought up the scenario of a rehire of a member that is of D-NCT status in Ariel DB and the rehire date is prior to the D-NCT date. In this instance (in EXO) we are providing an Informational message (ID 6874) to HOOPP in the CTL but Alias confirms this should be an Error and the employer should be made aware. We will bring Message ID 6874 within the Full Office Validation spec and HOOPP will have to designate a Master Val number to this validation.
Added “Message ID” section, as well as “Level” section to validations being covered in workshop of March 20, 2017.
“Section” refers to all interfaces to which the validation applies.
The dataimport message is the message that will contain parameters (if applicable, ex:  sometimes we extract a date or value from Ariel DB or file in the message, that is what is called a parameter).  We also need to provide a message for the Portal that does not include parameters for when validations are grouped by message on the Portal. If no parameters are present then both messages will be identical.
I just want to clarify that message I10 is looking at duplicate SINs within the same file. Is what expected for B1 to reject a member if that member is already active at the employer submitting data? Then if this is the case I do not believe there is a validation 
Can we remove the details that are in the HOOPP Additional Info from this document and the Master Val?

CS 20170512: 
do not include contributions with Summary Attribute = RRETRO or FRETRO coming from the file (only take what is in Ariel when it comes to retro transactions – do not take those in “create” mode)
this applies to to validations B9, B10 and B47 that call on the function : CalculateAEwithRetro

	Function
	
Function CalculateAEwithRetro (SIN, Employer, Year) {
If a SalaryRate record exists where
Type = REPORT
Year(EffectiveDate) = Year
Return SalaryRate.Rate
Else
Service = the SUM of Service.Amount of all Service records where 
Type = CTSRV
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
If (Service = 0) then:
return 0
End If
LowContributions = the SUM* of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = LowRPP
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
*do not include contributions with Summary Attribute = RRETRO or FRETRO coming from the file (only take what is in Ariel when it comes to retro transactions – do not take those in “create” mode)
HighContributions = the SUM* of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = HghRPP or HghRCA
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
*do not include contrinutions with Summary Attribute = RRETRO or FRETRO coming from the file (only take what is in Ariel when it comes to retro transactions – do not take those in create mode)

AE = ((LowContributions / LowContributionsRate) + (HighContributions / HighContributionsRate)) / (Service / 52)
Return AE
End If
End Function

	Function
	Function CalculatedAE for RetroYear (SIN, Employer, Year) {
If a SalaryRate record exists where
Type = REPORT
Year(EffectiveDate) = Year
Return SalaryRate.Rate
Else
Service = the SUM of Service.Amount of all Service records where 
Type = CTSRV
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
If (Service = 0) then:
return 0
End If
LowContributions = the SUM* of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = LowRPP
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
*do not include contributions with Summary Attribute = RRETRO or FRETRO coming from the file (only take what is in Ariel when it comes to retro transactions – do not take those in “create” mode)
HighContributions = the SUM* of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = HghRPP or HghRCA
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
*do not include contrinutions with Summary Attribute = RRETRO or FRETRO coming from the file (only take what is in Ariel when it comes to retro transactions – do not take those in create mode)

AE = ((LowContributions / LowContributionsRate) + (HighContributions / HighContributionsRate)) / (Service / 52)
Return AE
End If
End Function

	Validation – Validate Service Provided For Member on Free Accrual Entire Year
Validation Label 
	B22_MemberOnFreeAccrualEntireYearNoServiceRequired
	Message ID
	5604
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	CoreData (MDC), Events (Final Data), Previous Year Adjustment (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error 
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Service should not be reported for a member that is on free accrual for the entire MDC year.
Note: In case of amalgamation, the validation will consider all employments as the same one and look for the transactions that exist under the first employer FROM.   
	Validation Logic
	Previous Year Adjustment
Reject the member if:
File.SIN = Ariel.SIN
And
File.Employer = Ariel.Employer*
And
Ariel.Service Break = LTD where
Ariel.ServiceBreakStartDate <= January 1 of File.AdjustmentYear and
Ariel.ServiceBreakEndDate >= Dec 31 of  File.AdjustmentYear
And
File.Weeks > 0, or
File.LowContributions > 0, or
File.HighContributions > 0, or

*Note: In the case of amalgamation, the validation will account for any data reportedexisting service break reported for both the AFE employer and the ATE employer.

Core Data
Reject the member if:
File.SIN = Ariel.SIN
And
File.Employer = Ariel.Employer
And
Ariel.Service Break = LTD where
Ariel.ServiceBreakStartDate <= January 1 of Year Of Collect Start Date and
Ariel.ServiceBreakEndDate >= Dec 31 of  Year Of Collect Start Date 
And
File.Weeks > 0, or
File.LowContributions > 0, or
File.HighContributions > 0, or
File.PA > 0  

Events
Validation will have to be executed twice:

For current year:
Reject the member if 
File.SIN = Ariel.SIN
And
File.Employer = Ariel.Employer
And
{ 
Ariel.Service Break = LTD where
Ariel.ServiceBreakStartDate <= January 1 of Year Of  File.EmploymentEndDate or File.DeathDate, and
Ariel.ServiceBreakEndDate >=(File.EmploymentEndDate or File.DeathDate) 
And
File.Weeks_CurrentYear > 0, or
File. LowContributions_CurrentYear > 0, or
File. HighContributions_CurrentYear > 0, or
File.PA_CurrentYear > 0 
}
For previous year:
Reject the member if 
File.SIN = Ariel.SIN
And
File.Employer = Ariel.Employer
And
{
Ariel.Service Break = LTD where
Ariel.ServiceBreakStartDate <= January 1 of Year Of  File.EmploymentEndDate – 1year or File.DeathDate – 1year, and
Ariel.ServiceBreakEndDate >= (File.EmploymentEndDate – 1year or File.DeathDate – 1year) 
And
File.Weeks_PreviousYear > 0, or
File. LowContributions_PreviousYear > 0, or
File. HighContributions_PreviousYear > 0, or
}
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	This member is on a health leave and receiving free accrual during the reporting period. If the member has returned to work and resumed contributions in {2}, please report the Leave End Date.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)

Validation – Weeks For Part Time Member

Validation Label 
	B33_ Message
	Message ID
	5001
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Core Data, Final Data
	Validation Severity
	Warning
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to trigger if LSC (Lump Sum) exists for the Reporting year and member is PT at any point in the reporting year. 
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
Member is PT at least 1 day in target year
AND
For Core Data
There is an esistingexisting contribution transaction in Ariel DB
Where Contribution Type = [RPPLOW]
And Target Period Date = MDC year
Or
There is an esistingexisting service transaction in Ariel DB
Where Service Type = Contributory Service
And Summary Attribute Code = [RCL, RPREYAD, RRETRO]And Target Period Date = MDC year
For Events or Death current year
There is an esistingexisting contribution transaction in Ariel DB
Where Contribution Type = [RPPLOW]
And Target Period Date = the termination year
Or
There is an esistingexisting service transaction in Ariel DB
Where Service Type = Contributory Service
And Summary Attribute Code = [RCL, RPREYAD, RRETRO]And Target Period Date = the termination year - 1

For Events or Death previous year
There is an esistingexisting contribution transaction in Ariel DB
Where Contribution Type = [RPPLOW]
And Target Period Date = the termination year - 1
Or
There is an esistingexisting service transaction in Ariel DB
Where Service Type = Contributory Service
And Summary Attribute Code = [RCL, RPREYAD, RRETRO]And Target Period Date = the termination year - 1

	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	Lump Sum Contributions for {1} were previously reported for this member. Please verify that this service has been EXCLUDED. Select an override reason to continue.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Lump Sum Contributions were previously reported for this member. Please verify that this service has been EXCLUDED. Select an override reason to continue.
	Message Parameters
	1: Reporting year (YYYY)
	Warning Override Reasons
	Reported service does not include service for contributory leave.
	HOOPP Additional Info
	This validation should not trigger if the employer is entering a contributory leave during the annual process.

Validation – Low Conts Exceed Low Cont Tolerance

Validation Label 
	B37_ Message
	Message ID
	3029
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Core Data, Events (Final Data)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to prevent reported Low Contributions from exceeding Low Contribution Tolerance for the reporting year.  
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
Core Data
Low Contribution Variance > Low Contribution Tolerance 
Where;Low Contribution Variance = (Reported Low Contributions – Calculated Low Contributions)
WhereCalculated Low Contributions = [(YMPE *Low Contribution Rate)*Reported Weeks/52]
WhereLow Contribution Tolerance = (Max Weekly Low Contributions * Tolerance1) + Tolerance2 
WhereMax Weekly Low Contributions = [(YMPE *Low Contribution Rate)/52]
WhereTolerance1 = Defined valueTolerance2 = Defined value


Final Data
Low Contribution Variance > Low Contribution Tolerance 
Where;Low Contribution Variance = (Reported Low Contributions – Calculated Low Contributions)
WhereCalculated Low Contributions = [(YMPE *Low Contribution Rate)*Reported Weeks/52]
WhereLow Contribution Tolerance = (Max Weekly Low Contributions * Tolerance)
WhereMax Weekly Low Contributions = [(YMPE *Low Contribution Rate)/52]
WhereTolerance1 = Defined value

	Tolerance
	Tolerance1 = + 2 weeks of contributionsTolerance2 = +5$ of contributions
	DataImport Message
	Low Contributions for reporting year {1} cannot be greater than maximum amount of {2} for the weeks reported.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Low Contributions cannot be greater than maximum amount of weeks reported.
	Message Parameters
	1: Reporting year
2: Calculated Low+Tolerance1
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	a. Default tolerance = (+) 1 week
i. value should be a configurable parameter
b. This validation only applies to the conts and service reported in each form.  Data already loaded in Ariel is not to be considered.
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	YKN 20180709:
Changes for R17. Added a new tolerance of 5$ in the Low Contribution Tolerance for Core Data

Where;Low Contribution Variance = [(Reported Low Contributions + Low Contributions on Member Record1 for target year) – Calculated Low Contributions]
WhereCalculated Low Contributions = [(YMPE *Low Contribution Rate)*(Reported Weeks + Weeks on Member Record1  for target year)/52]
WhereLow Contribution Tolerance = (Max Weekly Low Contributions * Tolerance)
WhereMax Weekly Low Contributions = [(YMPE *Low Contribution Rate)/52]
WhereTolerance = Defined value
1 Include all contributions and service used to calculate AE with Transaction Indicator <> REGUL.

For PYA: In case an amalgamation occurred in a given year, the contributions and service for both the AFE and the ATE should be accounted for. 
	Tolerance
	+ 2 week of contributions
	DataImport Message

(without parameters)

	Message Parameters
	1: Reporting year
2: Calculated Low+Tolerance
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	a. Default tolerance = (+) 1 week
i. value should be a configurable parameter
b. This validation only applies to the conts and service reported in each form.  Data already loaded in Ariel is to be considered.

Validation – AE Increase Within Tolerance Compared to Previous Year
Validation Label 
	B40_AEIncrease
	Message ID
	1238
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Core Data (MDC- Member Data Collection), Previous Year Adjustment (Remittance), Events (Final Data), Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Warning
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to verify that Annualized Earnings (Reported or Calculated) for the reporting year is not greater than the AE (Stored or Calculated) for the reporting year -1 plus the tolerance.
Note: In case of amalgamation, the validation will consider all employments as the same one and look for the transactions that exist under the first employer FROM.   
	Validation Logic
	
Core Data (MDC- Member Data Collection)
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, Year(ExecutionParameter.StartDate)-1)  
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0
{
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, Year(ExecutionParameter.StartDate))

IF (AE_CurrentYear > (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) Error
}
Where:
Tolerance = 15%

Previous Year Adjustment (Remittance)
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.AdjustmentYear-1)  
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0
{
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.AdjustmentYear)

IF (AE_CurrentYear > (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) Error
}
Tolerance = 15%

Note: In case of amalgamation, the calculated AE will include service and contributions from the Employer FROM

Events (Final Data)
ReportingYear = Year(File.DateofDeath or File.EmploymentEndDate)
For ValidationYear = ReportingYear To ReportingYear-1 Step -1
{
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, ValidationYear -1)
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0 {
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, ValidationYear)
IF (AE_CurrentYear > (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) 
{
ErrorYear = Year(File.DateofDeath or File.EmploymentEndDate)
ErrorYear_CurrentAE = AE_CurrentYear
ErrorYear_PreviousAE = AE_PreviousYear
}
}
}
If ErrorYear <> null Error

Where:
Tolerance = 15%

Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.Year-1)  
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0
{
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.Year)

IF (AE_CurrentYear > (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) Error
}
Tolerance = 15%
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	The Annualized Earnings amount of ${0} is {1}% greater than the prior year's Annualized Earnings of ${2}. To continue, update the entry or select an override reason.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The Annualized Earnings amount for the reporting year is greater than the prior year's Annualized Earnings. To continue, update the entry or select an override reason.
	Message Parameters
	0: AE_CurrentYear
1: (AE_CurrentYear - AE_PreviousYear)AE_PreviousYear*100
2: AE_PreviousYear
	Warning Override Reasons
	1.           The member received a promotion
2.The member was compensated for additional responsibilities
3.Job reclassification
4.Contract settlement
5.Step/range increase
6.Contract settlement and step increase
7.Other - please provide explanation

	HOOPP Additional Info
	HOOPP has specified that the Tolerance be a configurable amount
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	TA 20210505:
Updated Validation Rule and Validation Logic to account for amalgamation.

MR 20180831: 
Following TASK0025406 condition added ‘Transaction Indicator <> Regul’.

MR 20180314:
XMO Validation brought into Full Office spec.

	Function
	Function CalculateAE (SIN, Employer, Year) {
If a SalaryRate record exists where
Type = REPORT
File.Employer = Ariel.Employer
Year(EffectiveDate) = Year
Return SalaryRate.Rate
Else
Service = the SUM of Service.Amount of all Service records where 
Type = CTSRV
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
If (Service = 0) then:
return 0
End If
LowContributions = the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = LowRPP
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO"
HighContributions = the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = HghRPP or HghRCA
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO"
AE = ((LowContributions / LowContributionsRate) + (HighContributions / HighContributionsRate)) / (Service / 52)
Return AE
End If
End Function

	

Validation – AE Increase Within Tolerance Compared to Previous Year HOOPP

If ErrorYear <> null Error

{

	1.Job reclassification
2.Permanent reduction of full-time hours
3.Change to lower-paying position
4.The member held more than one position at different hourly rates
5.Retro paid is Laboratory Medicine Funding Framework Agreement (LMFFA) compensation or pensionable bonus
6.Other - Please provide explanation

	HOOPP Additional Info
	HOOPP has specified that the Tolerance be a configurable amount

	Function
	Function CalculateAE (SIN, Employer, Year) {
If a SalaryRate record exists where
Type = REPORT
Year(EffectiveDate) = Year
Return SalaryRate.Rate
Else
Service = the SUM of Service.Amount of all Service records where 
Type = CTSRV
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
If (Service = 0) then:
return 0
End If
LowContributions = the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = LowRPP
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO"
HighContributions = the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = HghRPP or HghRCA
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO"
AE = ((LowContributions / LowContributionsRate) + (HighContributions / HighContributionsRate)) / (Service / 52)
Return AE
End If
End Function

	

Validation – AE Decrease Within Tolerance Compared to Previous Year HOOPP
Validation Label 
	B44_AEDecreaseHOOPP
	Message ID
	1646
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Core Data (MDC- Member Data Collection), Previous Year Adjustment (Remittance), Events (Final Data), Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Information
	Validation Visibility
	Private
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to verify that Annualized Earnings (Reported or Calculated) for the reporting year is not less than the AE (Stored or Calculated) for the reporting year -1 plus the tolerance.
Note: In the case of amalgamation, the validation will consider both employers as the same employer and use the dates to validate the right data
	Validation Logic
	Core Data (MDC- Member Data Collection)
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, Year(ExecutionParameter.StartDate)-1)  
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0
{
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, Year(ExecutionParameter.StartDate))

IF (AE_CurrentYear < (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) Error
}

Where:
Tolerance = -50000

Previous Year Adjustment (Remittance)
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.AdjustmentYear-1)  
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0
{
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.AdjustmentYear)

IF (AE_CurrentYear < (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) Error
}
Where:
Tolerance = -50000
Note: In case of amalgamation, the calculated AE will include service and contributions from the both the employers 
Events (Final Data)
ReportingYear = Year(File.DateofDeath or File.EmploymentEndDate)
For ValidationYear = ReportingYear To ReportingYear-1 Step -1
{
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, ValidationYear -1)
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0 {
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, ValidationYear)
IF (AE_CurrentYear < (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) 
{
ErrorYear = Year(File.DateofDeath or File.EmploymentEndDate)
ErrorYear_CurrentAE = AE_CurrentYear
ErrorYear_PreviousAE = AE_PreviousYear
}
}
}
If ErrorYear <> null Error

Where:
Tolerance = -50000

Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
AE_PreviousYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.Year-1)  
IF AE_PreviousYear <> 0
{
AE_CurrentYear = CalculateAE(File.SIN, Employer, File.Year)

IF (AE_CurrentYear < (AE_PreviousYear + Tolerance)) Error
}
Where:
Tolerance = -50000
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	Verify Earnings decrease greater than $50000
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Verify Earnings decrease greater than $50000
	Message Parameters
	N/A
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	HOOPP has specified that the Tolerance be a configurable amount

}
CalcYear++
}
if (AE_Calculate > 120,000 OR AE_Calculate < 20,000)) {
error
}

*In case of amalgamation, transactions linked to either the “EmploymentFrom” or the “EmploymentTo” will be considered:
CalcYear = Year(AmalgamatedFromEmployment.PermanencyDate)
while CalcYear < File.AdjustmentYear - 1 {

}

error
}

}
CalcYear++

error

If (CalculateAE(SIN, Employer, CalcYear) > 0) {
Exit function
}
CalcYear++
}
if (AE_Calculate > 120,000 OR AE_Calculate < 20,000)) {
error
}

Validation – Reported PA or Amended PA not within acceptable tolerance of the calculated PA for Non ltd members

Validation Label
	B53a_ReportedPAAmendedPANotWithinAcceptableToleranceCalculatedPANonLTD
	Message ID
	Core Date: 3508
Events: 2160
Previous Year Adjustment: 1623 
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section 
	Core (MDC), Previous Year Adjustments (Remittance), Contributory Leaves (Remittance) and Events
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Reported PA or Amended PA for NON-FA Mbr must be within the acceptable tolerance of the calculated PA.  

Note: In case of amalgamation, data from both the employers will be considered to determine the Calculated PA and Reported PA of both the employers will be considered to perform validations 
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
{
For Core Data
Member does not have a Service Break LTD in Ariel at any point in the MDC Year (based on Execution Start Date) 
For Previous Year Adjustments
Member does not have a Service Break LTD in Ariel during the adjustment year (based on File.AdjustmentYear)
For Contributory Leaves
Member does not have  a Service Break LTD in Ariel during the adjustment year (based on File.Year)
For Events
For Previous Year: Member does not have a Service Break LTD in Ariel during previous year (based on EmploymentEndDate-1 or DeathDate-1) 
For Current Year: Member does not have a Service Break LTD in Ariel during current year (based on EmploymentEndDate or DeathDate) 
}
and;
[(Calculated PA) – (File.PA)] ≤ -250
Or
[(Calculated PA) – (File.PA)] ≥ +250

Calculated PA = Min [Table PAMAXDB for current year*svc ;  (Max[0;(AnnualizedEarnings - YMPE)]*0.02*svc + min[AnnualizedEarnings;YMPE]*0.015*svc)*9 – Offset*svc ]

Where
Table «PAMAXDB» = Maximum PA for DB - Dependant on the year 
Svc = 1.0000 would mean 52 weeks. Svc dependant on the year. Should be the Weeks from the input file + weeks in Ariel1 for the corresponding year.
Annualized Earnings= Refer to Function Section below.
YMPE = Dependant on the year – you could refer to the rate table «MGA».
Pension Rate for earnings over YMPE = 0.020 (This is a fixed value, this is the pension rate determined by HOOPP)
Pension Rate for earnings up to YMPE = 0.015 (This is a fixed value, this is the pension rate determined by HOOPP) 
Offset (Post 1998) = 600 (this is determined by the Calculation Date). The rate table is « REDFE»*.
Offset (Pre 1998) = 1000 (this is determined by the Calculation Date). The rate table is « REDFE»*.
Note: In case of amalgamation, the calculated PA will include service and contributions from the Employer FROM
*Should point to the rate table.
1 Include all service withTransaction Indicator <> REGUL.
	Tolerance
	Default PA Tolerance = (+/-) 250
	DataImport Message

	The {2} for {3} is incorrect based on the data provided. The HOOPP calculated value is {4}.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Reported PA not in line.
	Message Parameters
	For Core Data:
2:  Word: PA
3:  Year(Execution Parameter.StartDate)
4:  CalculatedPA (rounded to whole number)
For Previous Year Adjustment:
2:  Word: Amended PA
3:  File.AdjustmentYear
4:  CalculatedPA (using formula as indicated above)
For Contributory Leaves (Remittance):
2:  Word: Amended PA
3:  File.Year
4:  CalculatedPA (using formula as indicated above)
Events:
2.  Word: PA
3:  File.Year(EmploymentEndDate or DeathDate) or File.Year(EmploymentEndDate)-1 or File.Year(Death Date)-1
4: CalculatedPA
	Warning Override Messages
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info

Validation – Reported PA or Amended PA not within acceptable tolerance of the calculated PA for ltd members

Validation Label
	B53b_ReportedPAAmendedPANotWithinAcceptableToleranceCalculatedPALTD
	Message ID
	Contributory Leaves: 9325
Core Data: 429
Events: 7375
Events_LTD: 8795
Previous Year Adjustment: 6035
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section 
	Core (MDC), Previous Year Adjustments (Remittance), Contributory Leaves (Remittance) and Events
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation Rule
	Reported PA or Amended PA for FA mbr must be within the acceptable tolerance of the calculated PA.  
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
{
For Core Data
Member has a Service Break LTD in Ariel at any point in the MDC Year (based on Execution Start Date) 
For Previous Year Adjustments
Member has a Service Break LTD in Ariel during the adjustment year (based on File.AdjustmentYear)
For Contributory Leaves
Member has a Service Break LTD in Ariel during the adjustment year (based on File.Year)
For Events
For Previous Year: Member has a Service Break LTD in Ariel during previous year (based on YearOf(EmploymentEndDate)-1 orYearOf(DeathDate)-1) 
For Current Year: Member has a Service Break LTD in Ariel during current year (based on YearOf(EmploymentEndDate) or YearOf(DeathDate) )
}
and;
[(Calculated PA) – (File.PA)] < 0
Or
[(Calculated PA) – (File.PA)] > 0

For FA (Free Accrual) members, these items in this formula:
Calculated PA = Min [Table PAMAXDB for current year * svc ;  (Max[0;(AnnualizedEarnings - YMPE)]*0.02*svc + min[AnnualizedEarnings;YMPE]*0.015*svc)*9 – Offset*svc ]

Should be replaced by: 

Situation 1 – the conditions are the following = 
LTD break starts in the year 
AND
LTD break’s start date is not Jan 1
AND
FARate6 does not exist for In-Year amalgamated members
AND
LTD break’s end date is on or after Jan 1 of the following year
                Then, annualized earnings is calculated this way :
                                Annualized Earnings based on Contributions1

Situation 2 – the conditions are the following = 
[
LTD break’s start date is on Jan 1 of the year or before
AND
LTD break’s end date is on or after Jan 1 of the following year
]
OR
[
LTD break starts in the year 
AND
LTD break’s start date is not Jan 1
AND
LTD break’s end date is on or after Jan 1 of the following year
AND
FARate6 exists for In-Year amalgamated members    
]
                Then, annualized earnings is calculated this way :
                                FARate6 

Situation 3 – the conditions are the following = 
(LTD break’s start date is on Jan 1 of the year or before
AND
LTD break’s end date is before Jan 1 of the following year)
OR
(LTD break’s start date is after Jan 1 of the year
AND
LTD break’s end date is before Jan 1 of the following year)
                Then, annualized earnings is calculated this way :
                                The AnnualizedEarnings will be the Max(Annualized Earnings based on Contributions1; Blended Earnings2)
Where:
Annualized Earnings based on contributions =
[Low RPP contributionsc transaction / 6.9% (rate that can be retrieved in the table LowContRate) + High RPP contributionsc transaction / 9.2% (rate that can be retrieved in the table HighContRate) + High RCA contributionsc transaction / 9.2% (rate that can be retrieved in the table HighContRate)] x 52 / contributory service transaction of the year
If   Year.Max(Annualized Earnings based on Contributions; Blended Earnings) = 0, Year - 1 transactions (service and contributions) should be used to calculate the Annualized Earnings. Iterations on each previous year will be done until max (Year where AE > 0;  Year.Employment.PermanancyDate) b (but it is implied that for situation #1, the Blended Earnings is equal to 0)
If Year.Employment.PermanancyDate.Annualized Earnings based on Contributions = 0;  then use 0. 
Blended Earnings =
Annualized Earnings based on Contributions1*(contributory service transaction/( contributory service transaction + FA service3)) + FARate6 * (FA service3/( contributory service transaction + FA service3))If FARate doesn’t exists, FARate = 0 is used.

FA service =
If the Calculated PA is for a year prior to 2017 = use the provided Free accrual service transaction (Service transaction with Service Type equal to Free Accrual Service)
If the Calculated PA is for 2017 or after  = FA service is calculated =   ACW Factor4 * (number of days member is on LTD break in the calendar year5)
ACW factor b =
If the LTD break start date is prior to 2017-01-01: ACW Factor is a service transaction.  The ACW Factor to be used will have the same start date as the LTD service break transaction.
If the LTD break start date is on or after 2017-01-01: The ACW factor will be calculated as per in Section 4.2 C of the Chapter 2 and consistent with Ariel DB.

Number of days member is on LTD break in the calendar year = 
Should be calculated based on the LTD transaction’s dates. Note that the end date of the transaction is excluded and the maximum date that should be used to calculate the number of days is min[termination date in the employment ; birth(+65,+0,+0)], and the maximum service that should be used to maximize the number of days is [35 – all contributory service transactions – FA service of all previous years].
FARate – in Salary Rate transactions – effective on the year with a Salary Rate Type equal to FARATE
The svc used in all situations should be =
                Contributory service transaction + FA service3 (but it is implied that for situation #2, the Contributory service is equal to 0)

Also where,
Table «PAMAXDB» = Maximum PA for DB - Dependant on the year 
Svc = 1.0000 would mean 52 weeks. Svc dependant on the year. Should be the Weeks from the input file + weeks in Ariela for the corresponding year.
Annualized Earnings= Refer to Function Section below.
YMPE = Dependant on the year – you could refer to the rate table «MGA».
Pension Rate for earnings over YMPE = 0.020 (This is a fixed value, this is the pension rate determined by HOOPP)
Pension Rate for earnings up to YMPE = 0.015 (This is a fixed value, this is the pension rate determined by HOOPP) 
Offset (Post 1998) = 600 (this is determined by the Calculation Date). The rate table is « REDFE»*.
Offset (Pre 1998) = 1000 (this is determined by the Calculation Date). The rate table is « REDFE»*.
*Rather than setting it as a fixed value, please have it point to the rate table.
a Include all service with Transaction Indicator <> REGUL.
b In the case of amalgamation, the validation will account for any data reported since the PermanencyDate of the AFE employer (AmalgamatedFromEmployment.PermanencyDate).
c Only Retro contributions PAID in the targeted Year should be used in AE calculation
	Tolerance
	Default PA Tolerance = 0
	DataImport Message
	The {2} for {3} with disability service is incorrect based on the data provided. The HOOPP calculated value is {4}.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Reported PA with disability service not in line.

	Amalgamation updates required

Type = LowRPP
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL

Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO" or “FRETRO”
LowContributions += the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = LowRPP
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(PaymentDate) = Year c
SummaryAttribute = "RRETRO" or “FRETRO”
HighContributions = the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = HghRPP or HghRCA
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO" or “FRETRO”
HighContributions += the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = HghRPP or HghRCA
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(PaymentDate) = Year c
SummaryAttribute = "RRETRO" or “FRETRO”
AE = ((LowContributions / LowContributionsRate) + (HighContributions / HighContributionsRate)) / (Service / 52)
Return AE
End If
End Function

I think the LAU only get set after annual calcs have been run, which can create a gap from when MDC data has actually been submitted and when the flag gets set.

Validation – Leave Start Date

Validation Label 
	B115_Message
	Message ID
	1645 (Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate is not null)
528 (Ariel.Employement.LastAnnualDataUpdate is null)
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Non-Contributory Leaves, Contributory Leaves
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to ensure that employers are not reporting new leaves for a year where the MDC data has already been reported.  
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :

If Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate is not null:
For a new leave (THE WHOLE LEAVE IS NEW)
File.LeaveStartDate is ≤ Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate

If Ariel.Employement.LastAnnualDataUpdate is null:
For a new leave (THE WHOLE LEAVE IS NEW)
     File.LeaveStartDate is ≤ Ariel.Employer.YearEndClosedIndicator

	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	If Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate is not null:
The leave start date must be later than {0}. Please review or contact HOOPP if this is correct.

If Ariel.Employement.LastAnnualDataUpdate is null:
The leave start date must be later than {1}. Please review or contact HOOPP if this is correct.

	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	If Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate is not null:
The leave start date must be later than the employment’s Last Annual Data Update date. Please review or contact HOOPP if this is correct.

If Ariel.Employement.LastAnnualDataUpdate is null:
The leave start date must be later than the employer’s year end closing date. Please review or contact HOOPP if this is correct.

	Message Parameters
	{0} : Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate (MM-DD-YYYY)
{1} : Ariel.Employer.YearEndClosedIndicator (MM-DD-YYYY)
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	A “new leave” is defined as: Leave that has NOT previously been reported (there is no matching leave with the same Leave Type and Leave Start Date).

Validation – Contributory Leave End Date Dec 31
Validation Label 
	B125_ContributoryLeaveEndDateDecember31
	Message ID
	2838
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Contributory Leaves (MDC Data Collection and Final Data)
	Validation Severity
	Warning
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	The Reported Contributory Leave End Date = Dec 31 of reporting year.
The Reported Leave End Date of the “associated” Non-Contributory Leave in Ariel > Dec 31, of reporting year OR open ended.
	Validation Logic
	If provided in the same data collection, the non-contributory leave file is always processed prior to the contributory leave file.

All the leaves considered as part of the conditions below would be applicable to the latest employment (Ariel.Employment) of the member (File.SIN) under the File.Employer.

This validation should be triggered if:

File.LeaveEndDate = 12-31-Year(ExecutionParameter.StartDate)
And
File.LeaveType in [01 (CRH), 02 (CRF), 03 (CRP), 03 (CRS), 05 (CRR), 06 (CRE), 07(CRO)]
AND
Ariel.LeaveEndDate should equal to Open Ended

The corresponding Ariel non-contributory leave is identified based on the following criteria:
File.LeaveStartDate = Ariel.LeaveStartDate
File.LeaveType (CL leave) matches Ariel.LeaveType (NCL leave):

File.LeaveType
	Ariel.LeaveType
	01 (CRH)
	NCH
	02 (CRF)
	NCF
	03 (CRP)
	NCP
	04 (CRS)
	NCS
	05 (CRR)
	NCR
	06 (CRE)
	NCE
	07 (CRO)
	NCO
	
Note: Additional details and examples are included in the HOOPP - Validation specifications - Annex Document

	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	You have reported a Contributory Leave End Date of {1}. If this is the actual leave end date please provide a Leave Of  Absence for this leave with a leave End Date of {1}.  Otherwise if the leave is continuing no further action is required.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	You have reported a Contributory Leave End Date of December 31. If this is the actual leave end date please provide a Leave Of  Absence for this leave with a leave End Date of December 31.  Otherwise if the leave is continuing no further action is required.
	Message Parameters
	{1}: File.LeaveEndDate {mm-dd-yyyy}
	Warning Override Reasons
	OK

Validation – Contributory Service cannot exceed period of non-contributory leave

Validation Label 
	B145A_ExceedPeriod
	Message ID
	5770
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Reported Service for the reporting year cannot exceed the period of the Non-Contributory Leave associated to the reporting year plus (ALT).  
	Validation Logic
	
LeaveStartDate = File.LeaveStartDate
LeaveEndDate = File.LeaveEndDate

// Adjust Start and End dates so the period we consider only covers the File.Year, even if the break starts and/or ends before or after the File.Year. //

If (File.LeaveStartDate < (File.Year)-01-01 {
LeaveStartDate = (File.Year)-01-01
}
If (LeaveStartDate > (File.Year)-12-31) {
LeaveStartDate = (File.Year)-12-31
}
If (LeaveEndDate < (File.Year)-01-01) {
LeaveEndDate = (File.Year)-01-01
}
If (LeaveEndDate > (File.Year)-12-31) {
LeaveEndDate = (File.Year)-12-31
}
BreakInDays = LeaveEndDate - LeaveStartDate
{ 
If BreakInDays = 0,  MaxService = 0
Else, If (BreakInDays > 0)  
OR BreakInDays = 0 and { LeaveStartDate = (File.Year)-12-31 or LeaveEndDate = (File.Year)-01-01 }

BreakInDays = BreakInDays + 1 

MaxService = Roundup(BreakInDays / [(File.Year)-12-31- (File.Year)-01-01 + 1] * 52, 2)  + Tolerance}

ReportedService = (File.TotalWeeksPaid * 100) / File.PctOfNormalEarnings
Tolerance = 3.00 weeks

Trigger an error If: ReportedService > MaxService 


	Tolerance
	Tolerance = (3) weeks
	DataImport Message
	The reported Weeks Paid for the {2} starting {3} exceed the {4} contributory weeks available for this leave in {5}.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The reported Weeks Paid for the Non-Contributory Leave exceed the contributory weeks available for this leave in the Reporting Year.
	Message Parameters
	{2} : Non-Contributory Leave Type
{3} : Non-Contributory Leave Start Date
{4} : Non-Contributory Leave Period
          {5} : Reporting Year
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	YK 20190923 : 
Update made to validation logic in order to target the File.Year provided only and make sure that the validation is triggered when the year provided is not included in the period covered by the leave selected. See VSTS 360160 & VSTS 360165.

MR 20180314:
XMO Validation brought into Full Office spec.

	

Validation – Contributory Service cannot exceed Multi-Year period of non-contributory leave
Validation Label
	B145b _MultiYearExceedPeriod
	Message ID
	5198
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Contributory Service cannot exceed period of non-contributory leave period associated to reporting year plus the Acceptable Leave Tolerance (ALT).
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if:
Prior to R15:

Year(File.StartDate) <> Year(File.EndDate)
AND
ServiceTotal > RoundUp(ServiceMax, 2) + Tolerance
 
Where :
{
ServiceTotal = 0
ServiceMax = 0
 
For every Transaction Record in the File with matching File.StartDate and File.EndDate
{
ServiceTotal = ServiceTotal + (File.TotalWeeksPaid/File.PctOfNormalEarnings) 
}
For every CalculationYear from Year(File.StartDate) to Year(File.EndDate) {
YearStart = Max(File.StartDate; (CalculationYear)-01-01)
YearEnd = Min(File.EndDate; (CalculationYear)-12-31)
ServiceMax = ServiceMax + (YearEnd - YearStart + 1) / ((CalculationYear)-12-31 - (CalculationYear)-01-01 + 1) * 52
}
}

As of R15:

Year(File.StartDate) <> Year(File.EndDate)
AND 
File.TotalWeeksPaid/File.PctOfNormalEarnings
>
RoundUp[{(LeaveEnd – LeaveStart + 1 day) / (Year(LeaveStart)-12-31 – Year(LeaveStart)-01-01 + 1 day}*52); 2]

Where: 
LeaveStart =
{ If Year(File.LeaveStartDate) < Year(File.Year)
= Year(File.Year)-01-01
Else 
= File.LeaveStartDate }

LeaveEnd=
{ If Year(File.LeaveEndDate) > Year(File.Year)
= Year(File.Year)-12-31
Else 
= File.LeaveEndDate }

***This should be done for each leave on the file (one by one ... nothing should be summed together)***

	Tolerance
	0 weeks
	DataImport Message

	The total Weeks Paid reported for the {1} starting {2} exceed the total amount of {3} contributory weeks available for this leave.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The reported Weeks Paid for the Non-Contributory Leave exceed the contributory weeks available for this leave in the Reporting Year.
	Message Parameters
	{1} : Non-Contributory Leave Type
{2} : Non-Contributory Leave Start Date 
{3} : Non-Contributory Leave Period
{4} : Reporting Year
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	N/A
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	MEP 20171219:
Added this validation into FO Spec following ALM 25669 and 25681. 
HOOPP to provide sign-off so updates can be made in R15. HOOPP to also confirm if a change is required to wording of message.

	

Validation – Contributory Service cannot exceed period of non-contributory leave

Validation Label 
	B145C_ExceedPeriod
	Message ID
	7197
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Information
	Validation Visibility
	Private
	Validation
Rule
	Reported Service for the reporting year can exceed the period of the Non-Contributory Leave associated to the reporting year as long as the excess does not exceed (ALT), but HOOPP needs to be informed.  
	Validation Logic
	LeaveStart = File.StartDate
LeaveEnd = File.EndDate

If Year(File.StartDate) <> Year(File.EndDate) {
If Year(File.EndDate)  <> File.Year {
LeaveEnd = Year(File.StartDate)-12-31
}
If Year(File.StartDate) <> File.Year 
LeaveStart = Year(File.EndDate)-01-01
}
}

If ((File.TotalWeeksPaid/File.PctOfNormalEarnings) – RoundUp((LeaveEnd – LeaveStart + 1 day) / (Year(LeaveStart)-12-31 – Year(LeaveStart)-01-01 + 1 day) * 52); 2) > 0
AND
((File.TotalWeeksPaid/File.PctOfNormalEarnings) – RoundUp((LeaveEnd – LeaveStart + 1 day) / (Year(LeaveStart)-12-31 – Year(LeaveStart)-01-01 + 1 day) * 52); 2) < Tolerance
{
Trigger error
}
	Tolerance
	Tolerance = 3.42* weeks
	DataImport Message
	The reported Weeks Paid for the {2} starting {3} exceed the {4} contributory weeks available for this leave in {5}. Please review Leave information.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The reported Weeks Paid for the Non-Contributory Leave exceed the contributory weeks available for this leave in the Reporting Year. Please review Leave information.
	Message Parameters
	{2} : Non-Contributory Leave Type
{3} : Non-Contributory Leave Start Date
{4} : Non-Contributory Leave Period
          {5} : Reporting Year
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info

Validation – Retro Contributions

Validation Label 
	B153_Message
	Message ID
	 1913
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Contributions Events
	Validation Severity
	Warning 
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation creates an exception if the Annualized Retro Amount for the retro year will increase the Base Annualized Earning in Ariel for the retro year by more than the acceptable Tolerance.
Note: In the case of amalgamation, the validation will consider both employers as the same employer and use the dates to validate the right data/transactions
	Validation Logic
	Annualized Retro Amount = Calculate Annualized Earnings from the File  
AND
Base Annualized Earnings = Calculate Annualized Earnings from Ariel
THEN
{
AEPctIncrease = ((Annualized Retro Amount + Base Annualized Earnings)/ Base Annualized Earnings) – 1) * 100

IF AEPctIncrease > 20%
Error
}
Note: In case of amalgamation, the calculated AE will include service and contributions from the Employer FROM
	Tolerance
	Tolerance = 20%
	DataImport Message
	The retro contributions reported for year {0} will increase the member’s annualized earnings by {1}%. Please verify the contributions and if correct, select the override reason.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The retro contributions reported for the Retro Year will increase the member’s annualized earnings. Please verify the contributions and if correct, select the override reason.
	Message Parameters
	0 = RetroYear
1 = AEPctIncrease
	Warning Override Reasons
	The reported Retro contributions are correct.
	HOOPP Additional Info
	
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	NS20210511:
Updated Validation Rule and Validation Logic to account for amalgamation

MR 20180831: 
Following TASK0025406 condition added ‘Transaction Indicator <> Regul’.

MR 20180314:
XMO Validation brought into Full Office spec.

	Function
	Function Calculate Annualized Earnings from Ariel (SIN, Employer, Year) {
If a SalaryRate record exists where
Type = REPORT
File.Employer = Ariel.Employer
Year(EffectiveDate) = Year
Return SalaryRate.Rate
Else
Service = the SUM of Service.Amount of all Service records where 
Type = CTSRV
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
If (Service = 0) then:
return 0
End If
LowContributions = the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = LowRPP
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO"
HighContributions = the SUM of Contributions.Amount of all Contributions records where 
Type = HghRPP or HghRCA
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
Member = SIN
Employer = Employer
Year(BeginDate) = Year
SummaryAttribute <> "RRETRO"
AE = ((LowContributions / LowContributionsRate) + (HighContributions / HighContributionsRate)) / (Service / 52)
Return AE
End If
End Function


Function Calculate Annualized Earnings from Ariel:
AE = ((Fie.LowContributions / LowContributionsRate) + (File.HighContributions / HighContributionsRate)) / (File.Weeks / 52)
Return AE

Validation – Waived Contributions For An Active Full-Time Member

Validation Label 
	B155_WaivedContributionsForActiveFullTimeMember
	Message ID
	 3566
	Validation Tool

	2nd level validation
	Section

	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error 
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Val B155b – Mbr cannot be FT and not contributing if the ER is actively participating in the plan.    
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member if:
For amalgamation cases*: Ariel.Employer = File.Employer, transactions linked to either the “EmploymentFrom” or the “EmploymentTo” will be considered

For other cases: Ariel.Employer = File.Employer

And
File.CurrentSIN = Ariel.SIN
And
{File.ContributoryStatus = NC
And
File.EmploymentType = FT}
Or
{File.ContributoryStatus = blank
And
File.EmploymentType = FT
And
Ariel.ServiceBreak = PTW , WSN or WSO
Where Ariel.EndDate = 12-31-2200 (open-ended ) of the most recent break}

Note: Regardless if concurrent or not, no check on other employments, unless the case being validated is an amalgamation scenario where both the “EmploymentFrom” and “EmploymentTo” will be considered.
* Amalgamation cases are identified by an employment record being linked to another employment record through the amalgamation links (seen in the employment screen in Ariel GUI
	Amalgamation updates required
	The validation will consider both employers as the same employer and therefore consider service breaks from both the AFE and ATE employer to identify if the member currently has an open-ended PTW, WSN or WSO break.

	N/A

	This member is not eligible to waive contributions. If the member is working full time, they must contribute to the Plan. Please correct the employment type and/or the contributory status.

(without parameters)

Postal code has a different value, Proposed values: '[1]T1R0G9, [2]T1A4A7, [3]T0J0Y4'  Original value: 'R1R 1R1'. 	Validation – Email Address Provided

Validation Label 
	B166_ EmailAddressProvided
	Message ID
	 454
	Validation Tool
	Standard Validation Module
	Level
	2nd level
	Section
	Enrolments
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error 
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Validation performed to ensure a valid email address has been provided. 
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member if :
*email address does not contain '@" sign;
and/or
*email address contains more than one "@"  sign that is not bound by quotation marks;
and/or
*local part of email address contains special characters that are not bound by quotation marks;
and/or
*email address contains quoted strings that are not dot separated or the quoted string is the only element making up the local-part of the email address;
and/or
*email address contains spaces, quotes, and backslashes that are not within quoted strings and preceded by a backslash;
and/or
*email  address contains double dot after @ sign
NOTE that according to the internationally accepted Application Techniques for Checking and Transformation of Names directive of the Network Working Group (2004) : 
“Without quotes, local-parts may consist of any combination of
   alphabetic characters, digits, or any of the special characters
      ! # $ % & ' * + - / = ?  ^ _ ` . { | } ~
   period (".") may also appear, but may not be used to start or end the
   local part, nor may two or more consecutive periods appear. .  Stated
   differently, any ASCII graphic (printing) character other than the
   at-sign ("@"), backslash, double quote, comma, or square brackets may
   appear without quoting. “ (p.6), the local-part being the part of the email before the “@”
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	Invalid email address provided.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Invalid email address provided.
	Message Parameters
	N/A
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	
	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	CS 20171101:
Precision added at the end of Validation Logic (NOTE that…) following reception of ALM 24261 / TFS 27738. 

AD 20170505:
Ready for sign-off.

Validation – Reported NCL Overlaps Existing NCL

Validation Label 
	B170_Message
	Message ID
	21
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Non-Contributory Leaves
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to prevent changing non-contributory leaves in specific scenarios. Non-Contributory Leave Reported overlaps with Non-Contributory Leave in Ariel.
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :

If Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate is not null:
Ariel.Employment = FileDerived.Employment
And 
{For all type of NC* leave overlaps Existing Break (any type including Health Leave “NCH”)
And Ariel.StartDate <> File.StartDate
And Min [File.BreakStartDate; Ariel.BreakStartDate] <= Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate
Or
If Ariel.Employment.LastAnnualDataUpdate is null:
Ariel.Employment = FileDerived.Employment
And 
{For all type of NC* leave overlaps Existing Break (any type including Health Leave “NCH”)
And Ariel.StartDate <> File.StartDate
And Min [File.BreakStartDate; Ariel.BreakStartDate] <= Ariel.Employer.YearEndClosedIndicator
Or;
{ For all type of NC* leave (any type excluding Health Leave “NCH”) overlaps Existing Break (= Health Leave “NCH”)}
Or;
{ Reported Break (= Health Leave “NCH”) overlaps Existing Break (= Health Leave “NCH”)
And 
(File.StartDate <> Ariel.StartDate
or
Ariel.ENDDate <> 12-31-2200)}

	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	The reported {1} starting {2: mm-dd-yyyy} and ending {3: mm-dd-yyyy} overlaps with previously reported:  {4} starting {5: MM-dd-yyyy} and ending {6: MM-dd-yyyy}. Please correct reported leave information or if the reported leave information is correct please contact HOOPP.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The reported non-contributory leave overlaps with a previously reported non-contributory leave. Please correct reported leave information or if the reported leave information is correct please contact HOOPP.
	Message Parameters
	1: {Reported Non-Contributory Leave Type}
2: {Reported Leave Start Date}
3: {Reported Leave End Date}
4: {Ariel Non-Contributory Leave Type}
5: {Ariel Leave Start Date}
6: {Ariel Leave End Date}
	Warning Override Reasons

Validation – Amended PA Change Tolerance

Validation Label 
	B173_ AmendedPAChangeTolerance (Removed)
	Message ID
	TBD
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Contributory Leave (Remittance), Previous Year Adjustment
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error 
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	a. If difference between (Amended PA in Ariel or PA in Ariel if no Amended PA in Ariel) and (Reported Amended PA) is less than the PA Tolerance.
i. Trigger validation if; 
1. [(Amended PA in Ariel or PA in Ariel if no Amended PA) – (Reported Amended PA)] < (+) PA Tolerance
And
2. [(Amended PA in Ariel or PA in Ariel if no Amended PA) – (Reported Amended PA)]> (-) PA Tolerance
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member if:
Ariel.PA.Amount - File.AmendedPA < 250
AND
Ariel.PA.Amount - File.AmendedPA > -250 

	Tolerance
	
	DataImport Message
	Please remove the Amended PA for {0}.  Based on the data provided an Amended PA does not need to be reported as the change in PA is less than ${1}.  
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Please remove the Amended PA for Reporting Year.  Based on the data provided an Amended PA does not need to be reported as the change in PA is less than PA Tolerance.  
	Message Parameters
	For Contributory Leave (Remittance):
0: Year
1: PA Tolerance=(+/-) 250

For Previous Year Adjustment:
0: Adjustment Year
1: PA Tolerance=(+/-) 250

	Warning Override Reasons
	None
	HOOPP Additional Info
	a. This validation should NOT trigger for the Amended PA if LAU (LAU at ER level) has NOT run for the reporting year.
i. LAU at ER level has been run if MDC or Final Event data has been submitted for the specific Mbr, at that specific ER, for that specific reporting year.  
b. This validation shall use Amended PA in Ariel if this value exists.  If Amended PA does NOT exist in Ariel, this validation shall use the PA in Ariel.
c. This validation should NOT trigger if the Amended PA value is blank/null.   
d. This validation shall validate the Reported Amended PA from the Remittance-PYA and Remittance-Contributory Leaves Data Files (if applicable). 
e. Default PA Tolerance = (+/-) 250
i. value should be a configurable parameter
k. The set of PA related validations should be triggered against “clean” data.
i. This validation should only be triggered if specific Mbr record data does NOT contain errors or warnings without warning overrides.
ii. This validation should be one of the last sets of validations triggered.
ii. If necessary, this validation should only be triggered when data collection is submitted.
f. If the PA change is less than $250 then an Employer does NOT need to report an Amended PA to CRA.  HOOPP wants to store what was reported to CRA. If we let ER report Amended PAs less than CRA tolerance that value will get stored in Ariel. Impact of not triggering this validation is that HOOPP will store a PA that is different than CRA reported value.   

	Updates
(History to be maintained)

Validation – Retro Paid in the Reporting Year

Validation Label 
	B181_RetroPaid
	Message ID
	6700
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Core Data (MDC), Events (Final Data)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Retro contributions were previously reported as paid in the reporting year, and no regular service or contributions have been reported for that reporting year (previously reported or being reported via the Data Collection or Event).  
	Validation Logic
	
Core Data (MDC)
At least one record in Ariel for the Reporting Year where: 
{
Ariel. Contribution.TransactionIndicator = RETRO
AND
Year(Ariel.Contributions.PaymentDate) = Year(ExecutionParameter.StartDate)
}AND
(File.PA  ≠0 AND File.Weeks = 0 AND  File.LowContributions = 0  AND File.HighContributions = 0) for the Reporting Year

Events (Final Data)
At least one record in Ariel for the Reporting Year where: 
{
Ariel. Contribution.TransactionIndicator = RETRO
AND
Year(Ariel.Contributions.PaymentDate) = Year(File.DateofDeath or File.EmploymentEndDate)
}
AND
(File.PA_CurrentYear < > 0 AND  File.Weeks_CurrentYear= 0 AND File.LowContributions_CurrentYear = 0 AND File.HighContributions_CurrentYear = 0) for the Reporting Year



	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	Retro Contributions have been previously reported as paid in {0} and no service or contributions have been reported for {0}.  Please report a PA of 0.00 and HOOPP will contact you with the corrected PA for {0}.  
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Retro Contributions have been previously reported as paid in Reporting Year and no service or contributions have been reported for Reporting Year.  Please report a PA of 0.00 and HOOPP will contact you with the corrected PA for Reporting Year.  
	Message Parameters
	Core Data
0: Year(ExecutionParameter.StartDate)
Events (Final Data)
0: Year(File.DateofDeath or File.EmploymentEndDate)
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info

Validation – Finalized PA/Amended Calculated

Validation Label 
	B183_FinalizedPA_2
	Message ID
	3430
	Validation Tool

	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Retro (Final Data, Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Information
	Validation Visibility
	Private
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to verify that retro is not being reported for a year paid that already has a finalized PA.
Note: In case of amalgamation, the validation will consider all employments as the same one and look for the transactions that exist under the first employer FROM. The PAs that were originally reported by the employer FROM will also be taken into consideration. 
	Validation Logic
	 
(File.RetroLowContributions > 0 OR File.RetroHighContributions > 0)
AND
At least one PA record exists where:
File.RetroYearPaid = Year(Ariel.PA.CalculationDate) -1

Note: 
“Year(Ariel.PA.CalculationDate) -1” is equal to “Year of Calculation” in Ariel GUI

*For Remittance only: 
In the case of amalgamation, the validation will account for any PA reported since the PermanencyDate of the AFE employer (AmalgamatedFromEmployment.PermanencyDate).
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message

	Validation – Excess Service for Members Enrolled Full-Year
Validation Label 
	B184a_ExcessServiceEnrolledFullYear
	Message ID
	66
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section 
	CoreData (MDC), Events (Final Data), Previous Year Adjustment (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation Rule
	Reported service cannot exceed the Full-Year Enrolment max possible service plus the acceptable tolerance.

Note: In the case of amalgamation, the validation will consider both employers as the same employer and use the dates to validate the right data/transactions.
	Validation Logic
	Set applicable Tolerance
ServiceTolerance = 0

Confirm Years validation is applicable for:
Validation Year is
For MDC Core Data = YYYY of Collect Start Date
For Previous Year Adjustment = File.AdjustmentYear
For Events Current Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate(TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate (DECFIN)
For Events Previous Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate – 1 (TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate - 1 (DECFIN)
Confirm member has been enrolled for the EntireYear
Ariel.Employment.PermanencyDate <= ValidationYear-01-01
AND
{ 
File.TerminationDate is null
OR
File.TerminationDate >= ValidationYear-12-31
}
Calculate Reported Service for the Reporting Year (File + Ariel) {
RS = SUM of Service.Service from all Service records (in Ariel or created by the Data Import) where :
{
Service.Type = CTSRV 
AND
Year(Service.TargetDate) = ValidationYear
AND
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL
}
Identify all Service Break and Part-Time periods that occurred during the year:

Create a temporary array “IdentifiedBreaks” containing, for all identified records, the following information :
ServiceBreak.StartDate
ServiceBreak.EndDate
Consider all ServiceBreak records where :
{
ServiceBreak.Type IN (LTD, NCM, NCU, , WSN, WSO, PTW, NCD)
AND
ServiceBreak.StartDate <= ValidationYear-12-31
AND
ServiceBreak.EndDate > ValidationYear-01-01
{

Trim any excess periods from the identified array: duplicates and/or dates that are out-of-range:
For each i record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
i.StartDate = MAX(i.StartDate; ValidationYear-01-01)
i.EndDate = MIN(i.EndDate; (ValidationYear + 1)-01-01)

For each additional j record in IdenfiedBreaks from (i+1)th  to Last
{
If (j.StartDate >= i.StartDate AND j.StartDate <= i.EndDate)
{
If (j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
j.EndDate = j.StartDate 
}
Else
{
i.EndDate = j.StartDate
}
}
Else
{
If (j.EndDate >= i.StartDate AND j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
i.StartDate = j.EndDate
}
Else If (j.StartDate < i.StartDate AND j.EndDate > i.EndDate)
{
i.EndDate = i.StartDate 
}
}
}
}
Calculate the length of all identified breaks:
CarveOutTotal = 0
For each record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
CarveOutTotal = CarveOutTotal + (i.EndDate – i.StartDate)
}
Calculated expected Service

TotalYear = (ValidationYear + 1)-01-01 – ValidationYear-01-01
TotalDays = TotalYear – CarveOutTotal

ES = ROUNDUP((TotalDays / TotalYear) * 52; 2)

Perform Validation

If (RS > ES + ServiceTolerance)
{
Generate an error
}
For PYA only: In the case of amalgamation, the validation will account for any data reported since the PermanencyDate of the AFE employer (AmalgamatedFromEmployment.PermanencyDate).
	Tolerance
	0 weeks
	DataImport Message
	Active full year: Total Weeks reported for {2} plus weeks previously reported exceed the {3} maximum possible weeks.         
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Active full year: Total Weeks reported for the reporting year plus weeks previously reported exceed the maximum possible weeks.         
	Auto Correct
	Calculated Amount = {3} – Ariel.Service (Type=CTSRV for applicable Validation Year)  
	Message Parameters
	2: {ValidationYear}
3: {ES}
Where Validation Year is
For MDC Core Data = YYYY of Collect Start Date
For Previous Year Adjustment = File.AdjustmentYear
For Events Current Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate(TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate (DECFIN)
For Events Previous Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate – 1 (TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate - 1 (DECFIN)
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	
	Updates
(History to be maintained)

Updated Validation Rule and Validation Logic to account for amalgamation

Validation – Excess Service for Members Enrolled Mid-Year.  
Validation label 
	B184b_ExcessServiceEnrolledMidYear
	Message ID
	3002
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section 
	CoreData (MDC), Events (Final Data), Previous Year Adjustment (Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation Rule
	Reported service cannot exceed the Mid-Year Enrollment max possible service plus the mid-year enrolment tolerance.  Note: In the case of amalgamation, the validation will consider both employers as the same employer and use the dates to validate the right data/transactions.
	Validation Logic
	Set applicable Tolerance
ServiceTolerance = 0

Confirm Years validation is applicable for:
Validation Year is
For MDC Core Data = YYYY of Collect Start Date
For Previous Year Adjustment = File.AdjustmentYear
For Events Current Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate(TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate (DECFIN)
For Events Previous Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate – 1 (TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate - 1 (DECFIN)

Set Work year start and end dates
YearStart = MAX(ValidationYear-01-01; Ariel.Employment.PermanencyDate)
YearEnd = MIN(ValidationYear-12-31; Ariel.Employment.TerminationDate)
Confirm member has been enrolled mid-Year
Ariel.Employment.PermanencyDate > ValidationYear-01-01
Calculate Reported Service for the Reporting Year (File + Ariel) {
RS = SUM of Service.Service from all Service records (in Ariel or created by the Data Import) where :
{
Service.Type = CTSRV 
AND
Year(Service.TargetDate) = ValidationYear
AND
Transaction Indicator <> REGUL}
Identify all Service Break and Part-Time periods that occurred during the year:

Create a temporary array “IdentifiedBreaks” containing, for all identified records, the following information :
ServiceBreak.StartDate
ServiceBreak.EndDate
Consider all ServiceBreak records where :
{
ServiceBreak.Type IN (LTD, NCM, NCU, WSN, WSO, PTW, NCD)
AND
ServiceBreak.StartDate <= YearEnd
AND
ServiceBreak.EndDate > YearStart
{

Trim any excess periods from the identified array: duplicates and/or dates that are out-of-range:

For each i record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
i.StartDate = MAX(i.StartDate; PermanencyDate)
i.EndDate = MIN(i.EndDate;Ariel.Employment.TerminationDate; (ValidationYear + 1)-01-01)

For each additional j record in IdenfiedBreaks from (i+1)th  to Last
{
If (j.StartDate >= i.StartDate AND j.StartDate <= i.EndDate)
{
If (j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
j.EndDate = j.StartDate 
}
Else
{
i.EndDate = j.StartDate
}
}
Else
{
If (j.EndDate >= i.StartDate AND j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
i.StartDate = j.EndDate
}
Else If (j.StartDate < i.StartDate AND j.EndDate > i.EndDate)
{
i.EndDate = i.StartDate 
}
}
}
}
Calculate the length of all identified breaks:
CarveOutTotal = 0
For each record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
CarveOutTotal = CarveOutTotal + (i.EndDate – i.StartDate)
}
Calculated expected Service

TotalYear = (ValidationYear-12-31 – ValidationYear-01-01) + 1
TotalDays = (YearEnd – YearStart + 1) – CarveOutTotal

ES = ROUNDUP((TotalDays / TotalYear) * 52; 2)

Perform Validation

If (RS > ES + ServiceTolerance)
{
Generate an error
}
For PYA only: In the case of amalgamation, the validation will account for any data reported since the PermanencyDate of the AFE employer (AmalgamatedFromEmployment.PermanencyDate).
	Tolerance
	0 weeks
	DataImport Message
	In-year enrolment: Total Weeks reported for {2} plus weeks previously reported exceed the {3} maximum possible weeks.         
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	In-year enrolment: Total Weeks reported for the reporting year plus weeks previously reported exceed the maximum possible weeks.

	Message Parameters
	2: {ValidationYear}
3: {ES}
Where Validation Year is
For MDC Core Data = YYYY of Collect Start Date
For Previous Year Adjustment = File.AdjustmentYear
For Events Current Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate(TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate (DECFIN)
