	2nd level validation
	Section 
	CoreData (MDC), Events (Final Data)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation Rule
	Reported service is within acceptable service shortfall tolerance and must be rolled up to minimum possible service.  
	Validation Logic
	Set applicable Tolerance
ServiceTolerance = -1

Confirm Years validation is applicable for:
For MDC Core Data = YYYY of Collect Start Date
For Events Current Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate(TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate (DECFIN)
For Events Previous Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate – 1 (TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate - 1 (DECFIN)
Confirm member has been enrolled for the EntireYear
Ariel.Employment.PermanencyDate < ValidationYear-01-01
AND
{ 
File.TerminationDate is null
OR
File.TerminationDate >= ValidationYear-12-31
}

Calculate Reported Service for the Reporting Year (File + Ariel) {
RS = SUM of Service.Service from all Service records (in Ariel or created by the Data Import) where :
{
Service.Type = CTSRV 
AND
Year(Service.TargetDate) = ValidationYear
}


Identify all Service Break and Part-Time periods that occurred during the year:

Create a temporary array “IdentifiedBreaks” containing, for all identified records, the following information :
ServiceBreak.StartDate
ServiceBreak.EndDate
Consider all ServiceBreak records where :
{
ServiceBreak.Type IN (PAR, LTD, NCH, NCF, NCP, NCS, NCR, NCE, NCO, NCU, NCM,WSN, WSO, PTW, NCD)
AND
ServiceBreak.StartDate <= ValidationYear-12-31
AND
ServiceBreak.EndDate > ValidationYear-01-01
{

Trim any excess periods from the identified array: duplicates and/or dates that are out-of-range:
For each i record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
i.StartDate = MAX(i.StartDate; ValidationYear-01-01)
i.EndDate = MIN(i.EndDate; (ValidationYear + 1)-01-01)

For each additional j record in IdenfiedBreaks from (i+1)th  to Last
{
If (j.StartDate >= i.StartDate AND j.StartDate <= i.EndDate)
{
If (j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
j.EndDate = j.StartDate 
}
Else
{
i.EndDate = j.StartDate
}
}
Else
{
If (j.EndDate >= i.StartDate AND j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
i.StartDate = j.EndDate
}
Else If (j.StartDate < i.StartDate AND j.EndDate > i.EndDate)
{
i.EndDate = i.StartDate 
}

}
}
}
Calculate the length of all identified breaks:
CarveOutTotal = 0
For each record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
CarveOutTotal = CarveOutTotal + (i.EndDate – i.StartDate)
}

Calculated expected Service

TotalYear = (ValidationYear + 1)-01-01 – ValidationYear-01-01
TotalDays = TotalYear – CarveOutTotal

ES = ROUNDDOWN((TotalDays / TotalYear) * 52; 2)


Perform Validation

If ((RS < ES) AND (RS ≥ ES + ServiceTolerance))
{
Generate an error
}

	Tolerance
	-1 weeks
	DataImport Message
	The Weeks reported for {2} should be adjusted to {3}. Contributions may not need to be adjusted if reported correctly.  
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The Weeks reported for Reporting Year should be adjusted to Minimum Possible Service. Contributions may not need to be adjusted if reported correctly.  
	Auto Correct
	Calculated Amount = {3} – Ariel.Service (Type=CTSRV for applicable Validation Year)  
	Message Parameters
	2: ValidationYear
3: ES
Where Validation Year is
For MDC Core Data = YYYY of Collect Start Date
For Events Current Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate(TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate (DECFIN)
For Events Previous Year = YYYY of File.EmploymentEndDate – 1 (TERFIN or RETFIN) or File.DeathDate - 1 (DECFIN)
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	
	Updates

Validation – Service Shortfall for Members Enrolled Mid-Year

Validation Label 
	B186b_Message
	Message ID
	3466
	Validation Tool
	Custom DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	CoreData (MDC), Events (Final Data)
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to verify that Reported service cannot be less than the Mid-Year Enrolment minimum plus the Mid-Year enrolment tolerance.
	Validation Logic
	
1-Confirm Years validation is applicable for:
ValidationYear = Year(ExecutionParameters.StartDate)

2-Set Work year start and end dates
YearStart = MAX(ValidationYear-01-01; Ariel.Employment.PermanencyDate)
YearEnd = MIN(ValidationYear-12-31; Ariel.Employment.TerminationDate)
3-Confirm member has been enrolled mid-Year 
Ariel.Employment.PermanencyDate > ValidationYear-01-01
AND
{ 
File.TerminationDate is null
OR
File.TerminationDate >= ValidationYear-12-31
}

4-Calculate Reported Service for the Reporting Year (File + Ariel) {
RS = SUM of Service.Service from all Service records (in Ariel or created by the Data Import) where :
{
Service.Type = CTSRV 
AND
Year(Service.TargetDate) = ValidationYear
}

5-Identify all Service Break and Part-Time periods that occurred during the year:

Create a temporary array “IdentifiedBreaks” containing, for all identified records, the following information :
•ServiceBreak.StartDate
•ServiceBreak.EndDate
Consider all ServiceBreak records where :
{
ServiceBreak.Type IN (PAR, LTD, NCH, NCF, NCP, NCS, NCR, NCE, NCO, NCU , NCM, WSN, WSO, PTW, NCD)
AND
ServiceBreak.StartDate <= YearEnd
AND
ServiceBreak.EndDate > YearStart
{

6-Trim any excess periods from the identified array: duplicates and/or dates that are out-of-range:
For each i record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
i.StartDate = MAX(i.StartDate; ValidationYear-01-01)
i.EndDate = MIN(i.EndDate; (ValidationYear + 1)-01-01)

For each additional j record in IdenfiedBreaks from (i+1)th  to Last
{
If (j.StartDate >= i.StartDate AND j.StartDate <= i.EndDate)
{
If (j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
j.EndDate = j.StartDate 
}
Else
{
i.EndDate = j.StartDate
}
}
Else
{
If (j.EndDate >= i.StartDate AND j.EndDate <= i.EndDate)
{
i.StartDate = j.EndDate
}
Else If (j.StartDate < i.StartDate AND j.EndDate > i.EndDate)
{
i.EndDate = i.StartDate 
}
}
}
}
7-Calculate the length of all identified breaks:
CarveOutTotal = 0
For each record in IdentifiedBreaks from 1st to Last
{
CarveOutTotal = CarveOutTotal + (i.EndDate – i.StartDate)
}

8-Calculated expected Service

TotalYear = (ValidationYear-12-31 – ValidationYear-01-01) + 1
TotalDays = (YearEnd – YearStart + 1) – CarveOutTotal

ES = ROUNDDOWN((TotalDays / TotalYear) * 52; 2)


9-Perform Validation

If (RS < MinimumService1)
{
Generate an error
}

1 – MinimumService is defined as follows:
For CoreData (MDC) : MinimumService = Minimum[0.65 * ES, ES+Tolerance (-3.42)]
For Events (Final Data) : MinimumService = ES+Tolerance (-3.42)
	Tolerance
	Tolerance = -3.42

MS 20170221:  
This validation was introduced following the reception of DB-Client Support # 7469. Employer’s submitted data using a default value of 01012016 for Employment Type Effective Date in order to bypass an existing validation (B24). As such, the Employment Type Effective Date was modified for everyone in the MDC files (even those for whom the B24 validation message was not triggered). The HOOPP service team noticed that the date was causing service breaks and other unpredictable results which could ultimately impact calculations downstream. 

	Validation – Retro Transaction Same Retro Year And Payment Year

Validation Label
	B191_RetroTransactionSameRetroYearAndPaymentYear
	Message ID
	 213
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Retro (Final Data and Remittance)
	Validation Severity
	Warning
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation triggered if ER is reporting a retro transaction with the same retro year paid and retro target year that has already been reported.  This validation is meant to inform ER that a similar retro transaction has already been reported and avoid the submission of a duplicate transaction from being reported. 

Note: In case of amalgamation, the validation will consider all employments as the same one and look for the transactions that exist under both employers FROM and TO.  
	Validation Logic
	Trigger warning if:
For amalgamation cases*: Ariel.Employer = File.Employer, transactions linked to either the “EmploymentFrom” or the “EmploymentTo” will be considered

For other cases: Ariel.Employer = File.Employer

And
Presence of File.RetroLowContributions is matched to an existing transaction type “Low” in Ariel with Transaction Indicator = RETRO
Where File. YearRetroPaid = Ariel.YearofPaymentDate
And
Where File. RetroYear = Ariel.YearofTargetDate
Or
Presence of File.RetroHighContributions is matched to an existing transaction type “High” in Ariel with Transaction Indicator = RETRO
Where File. YearRetroPaid = Ariel.YearofPaymentDate
And
Where File. RetroYear = Ariel.YearofTargetDate

* Amalgamation cases are identified by an employment record being linked to another employment record through the amalgamation links (seen in the employment screen in Ariel GUI
	Amalgamation updates required
	This validation is specific to the employer that provided the transaction. In the case of amalgamation, the validation will consider both employers as the same employer and use the dates to validate the right data/transactions.
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message

	Retro {word High or Low - Ariel} Contributions of ${Retro Amount - Ariel) for retro year {Retro Year-File} have been previously reported as paid in {Retro Year Paid- File}. If this is a duplicate retro transaction please remove. If the contributions reported are correct please, provide valid override reason.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	
Retro Contributions for the same Retro Year Paid and Retro Year have already been reported.
	Message Parameters
	TBD
	Warning Override Reasons
	Reported retro contributions are correct.
	Updates
(History to be maintained)

Changes for amalgamation to be implemented in a future release.

Validation – Duplicate MDC Data Received in Events File
Validation Label
	B192a_DuplicateMDCdataReceivedinEventsFile
	Message ID
	 405
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Events
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation triggered if ER is reporting duplicate MDC year data within Event Final Data (Term/Ret/Death).  This validation is meant to inform ER that MDC year data has already been reported and MUST be removed.     
	Validation Logic
	Reject member if:
Ariel.Employer = File.Employer
And
{
File. Weeks(PreviousYear)>0
Or
File.Low(PreviousYear)>0
Or
File.High(PreviousYear)>0
Or
File.PA(PreviousYear)>0
Or
File.AE(PreviousYear)>0
And
Ariel.ServiceRecord exists where of type = CTSRV
Ariel.SummaryAttribute = “MDC – Core Data” Year of Target Date = File.YearofEmploymentEndDate(or File.YearDeathDate)-1year 
}
Or
{
File. Weeks(CurrentYear)>0
Or
File.Low(CurrentYear)>0
Or
File.High(CurrentYear)>0
Or
File.PA(CurrentYear)>0
Or
File.AE(CurrentYear)>0
And
Ariel.ServiceRecord of type = CTSRV exists where 
Ariel.SummaryAttribute = “MDC – Core Data” Year of Target Date = File.YearofEmploymentEndDate(or File.YearDeathDate)
 }
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message

	Data for {0: YYYY} has already been reported. Please remove this data to continue.  If you need to adjust what was previously reported please contact HOOPP.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	Data has already been reported. Please remove this data to continue. If you need to adjust what was previously reported please contact HOOPP.
	Message Parameters
	{0}: YYYY - Previous Year (year of Employment End Date or Death Date minus 1)
{0}: YYYY - Current Year (Year of Employment End Date or Death Date minus 1)
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	
	Updates
(History to be maintained)

	Enrolments

	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to verify the format of the postal code.
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if:
The format of the postal code does not comply with the following:
Canada
The accepted formats are:
- A9A 9A9
- A9A9A9
- A9A-9A9
There are also restrictions on the first character of the postal code. It has to be within this group of possible values: {ABCEGHJKLMNPRSTVXY}
United States
- 99999
- 99999-9999
- 999999999
	Tolerance
	N/A

Wanted to add a precision raised by MS programmer: this message can only occur when there are two or more members in the database with the same SIN as member being processed in interface file. Validation – B214

Validation Label 
	B214
	Message ID
	6012
	Validation Tool
	Data Import, DLL
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Core Data and Events
	Validation Severity
	Warning
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Member is Part-Time at any point in target year and has a Non-contributory leave on file.  Weeks being reported extend into the non-contributory leave period.  Employer should be reporting a contributory leave data. 
	Validation Logic
	This validation will be triggered If :
The provided weeks exceed the working contributory weeks available for this member in the reporting year:
Core Data
Member is PT at least 1 day in target year
AND
There is a non-contributory leave1 in target year for at least 5 days
AND
File.CoreData.Weeks > WorkingContributoryPeriod + Tolerance
Where:
WorkingContributoryPeriod = ROUNDDOWN (((Number of days between (BeginDate, EndDate) - “Carve Out” service breaks as defined in Service Break Defintions) / (Number of days between (BeginDate, EndDate)) *52,2)
This includes contributory leaves not reported through MDC data collection and non contributory service leaves reported in the data collection.
BeginDate = The most recent date between the 1st January of the MDC year and Ariel.PermanencyDate
EndDate = Should be set to January 1st of the MDC year+1
Service Breaks3 = NCE, NCF, NCP, NCS, NCM, NCD, NCO, NCU, NCR, NCH, WSN, WSO, LTD, PTW
Or
Service Breaks3 = CRH, CRE, CRF, CRP, CRS, CRM, CRO, CRR and was not reported through MDC data collection2

Events or Death current year
Member is PT at least 1 day in target year
AND
There is a non-contributory leave1 in target year for at least 5 days
AND
File.Events.WeeksCurrentYear  > WorkingContributoryPeriod + Tolerance
Where:
WorkingContributoryPeriod = ROUNDDOWN (((Number of days between (BeginDate, EndDate) - “Carve Out” service breaks as defined in Service Break Defintions) / (Number of days between (BeginDate, EndDate)) *52,2)
This includes contributory leaves not reported through Final Data data collection and non contributory leaves reported in the data collection.
BeginDate = The most recent date between the 1st January of the termination year and Ariel.PermanencyDate
If EventType = TERFIN Or RETFIN
EndDate = File.Events.EmploymentEndDate+1 day
If EventType = DECFIN
EndDate = File.Events.DateOfDeath
Service Breaks3 = NCE, NCF, NCP, NCS, NCM, NCD, NCO, NCU, NCR, NCH, WSN, WSO, LTD, PTW
Or
Service Breaks3 = CRH, CRE, CRF, CRP, CRS, CRM, CRO, CRR and was not reported through Final Data data collection2

Events or Death previous year
Member is PT at least 1 day in target year-1
AND
There is a non-contributory leave1 in target year-1 for at least 5 days
AND
File.Events.WeeksPreviousYear > WorkingContributoryPeriod + Tolerance
Where:
WorkingContributoryPeriod = ROUNDDOWN (((Number of days between (BeginDate, EndDate) - “Carve Out” service breaks as defined in Service Break Defintions) / (Number of days between (BeginDate, EndDate)) *52,2)
This includes contributory leaves not reported through not reported through Final Data data collection and non contributory leaves reported in the data collection.
BeginDate = The most recent date between the 1st January of termination year - 1 and Ariel.PermanencyDate
EndDate = Should be set to January 1st of Termination year
Service Breaks3 = NCE, NCF, NCP, NCS, NCM, NCD, NCO, NCU, NCR, NCH, WSN, WSO, LTD, PTW
Or
Service Breaks3 = CRH, CRE, CRF, CRP, CRS, CRM, CRO, CRR and was not reported through Final Data data collection2

A non-contributory leave is a service break with one of the following codes : NCE, NCF, NCP, NCS, NCR, NCO, NCH

A non-contributory leave being flipped to contributory in the current data collection will not be carved out of the working contributory period due to the additional condition on the data collection through which the contributory leave is being reported. Since the leaves will be processed prior to the core data file of events file, we consider the leave information being reported as being already present in Ariel which explains that no additional condition on the leave information being reported is necessary.

The leave period being carved out will cannot be outside of the start and end dates of the Target Year. In other words, the leave period to carve out will be defined as follows:
LeaveStartDate to Carve Out: Maximum (LeaveStartDate, BeginDate)
LeaveEndDate to Carve Out: Minimum (LeaveEndDate, EndDate)

	Tolerance
	0.14 weeks
	DataImport Message
	This member is part-time with a leave on file for which a contributory leave applies. Please complete the contributory leave information for {1} or select an override reason.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	This member is part-time with a leave on file for which a contributory leave applies. Please complete the contributory leave information for this member or select an override reason.
	Message Parameters
	For Core Data:
1:  Year(Execution Parameter.StartDate)
Events:
1:  File.Year(EmploymentEndDate or DeathDate) or File.Year(EmploymentEndDate)-1 or File.Year(Death Date)-1

	Warning Override Reasons
	Contributory leave data reported.
Member did not contribute for this leave within the reporting period.
	HOOPP Additional Info
	Val 145d should be displayed before new Val B214.  If ER reports too much contributory leave data (more than is available for the leave), Employer should see that validation first.
Please note: Final data reporting does not have val 145e, which validates that reported contributory leave data does not exceed availalbe non-contributory leave period.

	Updates
(History to be maintained)
	YKN 20190201:
Added the message ID

YKN 20180910:
Added a parameter following HOOPP’s (Kate) approval on September 7th 
YKN 20180711:
New validation for R17. See ALM 28826
	Function
	Working Contributory Period = [(sum of days between the Jan 1 or if enrolled in target year then enrolment date and Dec 31 of Target Year or if terminated in target year then termination date) minus (“Carve Out* service breaks as defined in Service Break Defintions: Working Contributory Period) - this includes service breaks reported in the file].  Note: Convert weeks based current methodolgy.

*Carve Out methodology should be based on dates and should only excldue a time period once even if service breaks overlap - the overlapping dates should not be accounted for twice.

	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	Specific validation to ensure the Leave End Date is after the Leave Start Date.
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
Ariel.Employment = FileDerived.Employment
And
File.LeaveEndDate is not null
AND
Ariel.LeaveStartDate > File.LeaveEndDate
Note : The leave types considered as to be opened should have the following codes NCH; NCF; NCP; NCS; NCR; NCE; NCO; NCU; LTD; NCD; NCM.
	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	The Leave End Date is before the Leave Start Date. Please revise data.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	The Leave End Date is before the Leave Start Date. Please revise data.
	Message Parameters
	N/A
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	
	Updates
(History to be maintained)

Updated applicable leave types.
YKN 20180711:
New validation for R17. See ALM 29058.
	
Validation – NHH Member

Validation Label 
	B223_ NHH_MEMBER
	Message ID
	2953
	Validation Tool
	Data Import
	Level
	2nd level validation
	Section
	Events, Remittance(Contributory Leaves, Previous Year Adjustments, Retro), MBI, Retirement Notice
	Validation Severity
	Complete Member Error
	Validation Visibility
	Public
	Validation
Rule
	If an NHH employer initiates an event via the HOOPP Insight for an event date that is prior to the merger effective date, or if any retroactive member information changes (including retro contributions, previous year adjustments, & lump sum contributions) are submitted for NHH members via the employer portal for a date that is prior to the merger effective date; the employer should get an error message and should not be allowed to submit the request via the HOOPP Insight
	Validation Logic
	Reject the member in the input file if :
File.Employer = Sisters of St. Josephs, Providence, St. Joseph’s, Grand River Hospital, St. Michael’s Healthcare
And
Membership.CalculationIndicator.IndicatorCode = NHHSIS, NHHSTJ, NHHPRO, NHHSTM, NHHGR
And
 (
Events (Current Year and Previous Year)
FileDerived.Service.TargetDate < MergerEffectiveDate1
Or
FileDerived.Contribution.TargetDate < MergerEffectiveDate1

Contributory Leaves (Remittance)
FileDerived.Service.TargetDate < MergerEffectiveDate1
Or
FileDerived.Contribution.TargetDate < MergerEffectiveDate1

Previous Year Adjustments
FileDerived.Service.TargetDate < MergerEffectiveDate1
Or
FileDerived.Contribution.TargetDate < MergerEffectiveDate1

Retro
FileDerived.Contribution.TargetDate < MergerEffectiveDate1

Member Basic Information
File.ContributoryStatusEffectiveDate < MergerEffectiveDate1

Retirement Notice
File.EmploymentEndDate < MergerEffectiveDate1
)
1The merger effective date is different for each employer and is determined as follows:
Indicator code
	Employer name
	MergerEffectiveDate
	NHHSIS
	Non Hoopp Hospital member - Sisters of St-Joseph
	2019-01-01
	NHHSTJ
	Non Hoopp Hospital member - St-Joseph
	2019-01-01
	NHHPRO
	Non Hoopp Hospital member - Providence
	2019-01-01
	NHHSTM
	Non Hoopp Hospital member - St-Mikes
	2019-07-01
	NHHGR
	Non Hoopp Hospital member - Grand River
	2019-06-01
	
Sisters of St. Joseph's
	0359
	Providence
	0235
	Grand River
	0135
	St. Joseph’s
	0235
	St. Michael’s Healthcare
	0235
	 

	Tolerance
	N/A
	DataImport Message
	This event cannot be submitted via HOOPP Insight. Please contact HOOPP for assistance.
	Portal Message
(without parameters)
	This event cannot be submitted via HOOPP Insight. Please contact HOOPP for assistance.
	Message Parameters
	N/A
	Warning Override Reasons
	N/A
	HOOPP Additional Info
	This would include anything submitted via a member event (retirement/termination/pre-retirement) or data feed/ data collection/file import.
